## Best date plans

### 2026-11-28 — Found Sound Nation: Mara Rosenbloom + Selendis S.A. Johnson + WINNER Bakery

**Found Sound Nation: Mara Rosenbloom + Selendis S.A. Johnson, then dinner.** Bakery and sandwiches at WINNER Bakery. It is a 6-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 4 pm, so eat afterwards.

### 2026-11-23 — Roebling Rink: Monday Skating + Walter's

#### [Lightscape](https://www.bbg.org/lightscape)

Time to confirm · outdoors · Off-peak date; adult fare and fees must be checked when choosing a session · Brooklyn Botanic Garden · Crown Heights · Brooklyn · Score 72.0  
A mile-long outdoor route through the garden animated by seasonal light installations, color and sound.
**Timing:** Evening timed-entry attraction. November 22 is confirmed open; select an available entry time at checkout.
**Practical note:** Allow 45-75 minutes. Rain is forecast; the trail normally runs rain or shine, with safety cancellations announced by the organizer.
**Why now:** The official 2026 calendar lists November 22 as an operating night.
**On-sale watch:** Separate Lightscape tickets and a timed entry are required.

#### [Andy Warhol’s 'Screen Tests' + Conrad Ventur’s 'Screen Tests Revisited', Pgm 2](https://www.anthologyfilmarchives.org/film_screenings/calendar?view=list&month=11&year=2026#showing-62295)

Time to confirm · film · Anthology Film Archives · East Village · Manhattan · Score 57.0  
These two programs pair Warhol’s seminal and often exquisite SCREEN TESTS – the silent, black-and-white, three-and-a-half minute filmic portraits he made between 1964-66, which grew to encompass 472 separate reels, portraying 189 individual subjects (many of them sitting for multiple portraits) – with Conrad Ventur’s 21st-century remakes. ## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 22 and the weeks ahead: neighborhood deals, recent openings, Thanksgiving menus and dated chef collaborations. Check each schedule; not every offer is available today.

Dining research: 2026-11-22T10:39:00+00:00

No current Michelin stars are asserted. Gui's captured website describes it as Michelin Selected, but its linked Michelin page returned no readable content: https://guide.michelin.com/us/en/new-york-state/new-york/restaurant/gui-1207502. Theodora's organizer description says Michelin-recommended, not starred. Junoon's historical star claims do not establish a current award. Recognition fields remain empty.

### $1 morning coffee at the newly opened Mercato Centrale

Deals & happy hours — Mercato Centrale

The new Grand Central market offers an inexpensive morning stop alongside sandwiches, salads and prepared foods.

Price: $1 La Colombe drip coffee  
Schedule: Daily, 7–11 a.m.; regular market hours 7 a.m.–11 p.m.  
Conditions: One cup per person. Walk-in, self-checkout market. Additional-purchase requirements and tax treatment are not stated. No promotion end date or holiday exception was found.  
Opened: 2026-11-17  
[Source](https://www.centralenyc.com/mercato)

### Mermaid Inn Chelsea: $2 happy-hour oysters and clams

Deals & happy hours — The Mermaid Inn Chelsea

Current happy-hour choices include shellfish, $4 mini fish tacos and $4 mini clam chowder, with selected discounted drinks.

Price: Chef's-choice oysters and littleneck clams $2 each, minimum six; selected cocktails $12, wine $10, beer $8  
Schedule: 4:30–6 p.m.; the official happy-hour menu does not specify days.  
Conditions: Shellfish selection is chef's choice; minimum six applies. Reservation requirement, tax, gratuity and service charges are not stated. No holiday closure notice appeared on the checked location page.  
[Source](https://themermaidnyc.getbento.com/the-mermaid-inn-chelsea-menus/)

### Junoon's $43 two-course or $49 three-course lunch

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

The Prestige lunch includes basmati rice and naan. Choices include eggplant chaat, chicken gassi, mushroom xacutti and squash coconut curry.

Price: Two courses $43; three courses $49  
Schedule: Lunch; the official location FAQ lists Friday–Sunday, 11:45 a.m.–2:30 p.m.  
Conditions: Reservations recommended. Official policy welcomes children over 10 and requests smart-casual dress, excluding gym shorts, tank tops and flip-flops. Menu states 20% service charge for parties of five or more. Tax and other gratuity treatment are unstated. Lamb chops have a separate $7 notation; confirm its application. Holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

Choose a vegetable starter, a main such as pesto fusilli or a meatball-Parmesan hero, and gelato or sorbet.

Schedule: Monday–Friday, 11:30 a.m.–3 p.m.; not a Sunday November 22 offer  
Conditions: Limited prix-fixe selections, not the full à-la-carte menu. Reservation requirement, beverages, tax, gratuity and holiday exclusions are not stated. Thanksgiving participation is unconfirmed.

Special menus — Junoon

An ongoing themed tasting moves through regional preparations including Kashmiri haak, Tamil Nadu uttapam and Bengali paturi, with vegetarian alternatives listed for several courses. This is not a newly announced event and is not available Sunday, November 22.

Price: Dedicated Journey Through India menu: $165 per person; optional wine pairing $95. Generic location copy separately advertises a $115 seven-course tasting; discrepancy unresolved.  
Schedule: Dinner tasting menu, excluding Sundays; precise service hours unverified.  
Conditions: Whole-table participation required. The menu states a 20% service charge for parties of five or more. Tax treatment, additional gratuity, reservation requirement and holiday exceptions are not specified. Sunday exclusion applies to this tasting, not necessarily the restaurant.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)
